#14 [10/15] RUN printf '#!/bin/sh\n# Test runner that intercepts npm test command\n# When called as "npm test -- packages/anthropic-ai/src/index.spec.ts"\n# we need to extract the test file and run jest directly\n\n# Check if we were called with test file arguments\nTEST_FILE=""\nfor arg in "$@"; do\n    if echo "$arg" | grep -q "^packages/.*\\.spec\\.ts$"; then\n        TEST_FILE="$arg"\n        break\n    fi\n    if echo "$arg" | grep -q "^packages/.*\\.test\\.ts$"; then\n        TEST_FILE="$arg"\n        break\n    fi\n    if echo "$arg" | grep -q "\\.spec\\.ts$"; then\n        TEST_FILE="$arg"\n        break\n    fi\n    if echo "$arg" | grep -q "\\.test\\.ts$"; then\n        TEST_FILE="$arg"\n        break\n    fi\ndone\n\nif [ -n "$TEST_FILE" ]; then\n    # Extract package name from test file path\n    PKG_NAME=$(echo "$TEST_FILE" | cut -d/ -f2)\n    REL_PATH=$(echo "$TEST_FILE" | sed "s|^packages/$PKG_NAME/||")\n    \n    echo "Running specific test: $TEST_FILE"\n    echo "Package: $PKG_NAME"\n    echo "Test path: $REL_PATH"\n    \n    # Change to package directory and run jest directly\n    cd "/app/packages/$PKG_NAME" && \n    npx jest "$REL_PATH" --passWithNoTests\n    exit $?\nelse\n    # No specific test file provided, run all tests using lerna but ignore root package\n    echo "Running all tests with lerna (ignoring root)..."\n    cd /app && npx lerna run test --stream --ignore voltagent\n    exit $?\nfi\n' > /app/run-test.sh && chmod +x /app/run-test.sh
#14 DONE 0.5s

#15 [11/16] RUN printf '#!/bin/sh\n# Simple test runner that handles the test harness command format\n# When called as "npm test -- packages/anthropic-ai/src/index.spec.ts"\n# we need to extract the test file and run jest directly\n\n# Extract test file from arguments\nTEST_FILE=""\nfor arg in "$@"; do\n    if echo "$arg" | grep -q "\\.spec\\.ts$" || echo "$arg" | grep -q "\\.test\\.ts$"; then\n        TEST_FILE="$arg"\n        break\n    fi\ndone\n\nif [ -n "$TEST_FILE" ]; then\n    # Get the package directory (second component of path)\n    PKG_DIR=$(echo "$TEST_FILE" | cut -d/ -f2)\n    # Get the relative path within the package\n    REL_PATH=$(echo "$TEST_FILE" | cut -d/ -f3-)\n    \n    echo "Running test: $TEST_FILE"\n    echo "Package directory: $PKG_DIR"\n    echo "Relative path: $REL_PATH"\n    \n    # Change to the package directory and run jest\n    cd "/app/packages/$PKG_DIR" && \\\n    npx jest "$REL_PATH" --passWithNoTests\n    exit $?\nelse\n    # No specific test file, run all tests\n    echo "Running all tests with lerna..."\n    cd /app && npx lerna run test --stream\n    exit $?\nfi\n' > /app/run-test.sh && chmod +x /app/run-test.sh
